## [0.11.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift/compare/v0.10.0...v0.11.0) (2026-08-26)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **launcher:** `spindrift doctor` no longer exits a flat 0/1 -- it now exits 0 healthy, 1 reserved for internal errors, 2 configuration invalid, 3 auth/connectivity, or 4 required checks failed or declined.
* **roster:** a roster entry with an unknown key, a missing `model` key, or an unresolvable promptFile now fails eval instead of being silently accepted or degraded. See MIGRATING.md.
* **versioning:** agent-trigger is no longer part of the versioned label lifecycle contract in VERSIONING.md. It may change name in a future non-major release; the label's own runtime behavior in agent-dispatch.yml is unaffected.
* **mkHarness:** a direct mkHarness caller passing a choices-bearing knob's default = null now fails eval (previously silently accepted and rendered as an empty string, e.g. MERGE_METHOD=""). See MIGRATING.md.
* **mkHarness:** mkHarness now throws if a choices-bearing knob's merged default is not a member of its declared choices. A direct mkHarness caller (bypassing the flake module) passing an invalid value, e.g. mkHarness { defaults.mergeMethod = "Auto"; }, previously rendered silently into the Launcher input document; it now fails eval instead. See MIGRATING.md.
* **flakeModule:** Consumer flake options for the seven choices-bearing knobs (mergeMode, codeForge, issueTracker, overlapGate, mergeMethod, syncMethod, boxForgeAndIssueAccess) now use types.enum instead of types.str. A Consumer setting one to a value outside its choices list (e.g. git.merge.policy = "Auto") now fails nix eval instead of evaluating. See MIGRATING.md.
* **entrypoint:** REVIEW_EFFORT/--review-effort no longer take effect as a live, per-dispatch Box-runtime override -- setting either at dispatch time is now a silent no-op. The value now reaches the orchestrator only through the nix-baked roster reviewer entry's own effort (settings.models.reviewEffort, image rebuild required), the same shape REVIEW_MODEL already has. Under DRIVER=opencode specifically, whose agentsJsonTemplate never renders a reviewer key, the review pass now always falls back to the coordinator's own effort -- matching REVIEW_MODEL's existing opencode fallback. See MIGRATING.md.

## [0.10.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift/compare/v0.9.0...v0.10.0) (2026-08-15)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **roster:** an unmentioned agent in defaultRoster's models (or one whose legacy positional knob is unset) now inherits its lib/env-schema.nix schema default instead of staying blank. A caller relying on the old blank-by-default behavior must set models.<name> = "" explicitly to keep opting that agent out.

## [0.6.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift/compare/v0.5.1...v0.6.0) (2026-07-20)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **console:** the live-tail sidebar now advances a running Dispatch's Activity feed on its own instead of staying frozen at whatever it showed on open, opens at the bottom of the feed instead of the top, and gains new keys (G/end to re-attach follow, z to toggle fullscreen zoom).
* **console:** the Console's drill-in keymap changes. Enter opens a docked sidebar (fullscreen only when the terminal is too narrow) instead of always going fullscreen; "t" cycles Activity feed -> Transcript -> raw JSONL instead of a plain rendered/raw toggle; "h"/ "l" and left/right now move focus between the list and the sidebar.
* **console:** the Console keymap changes for anyone driving a session interactively. Tab (switch backlog/queue focus) and the docked/floating/fullscreen pane-mode key are removed; Terminate moves from "k" to "X"; "i" no longer moves the cursor up (use "k"). The body now shows one Section at a time instead of two columns side by side, navigated with H/L (previous/next Section) and 1-5 (jump directly).

## [0.4.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift/compare/v0.3.0...v0.4.0) (2026-07-12)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **settings:** settings.concurrency.depsPollSecs/depsWaitSecs (DEPS_POLL_SECS/DEPS_WAIT_SECS) are removed. A consumer that still sets either gets an unknown-key eval error naming the valid keys, per the pre-1.0 policy (ADR 0010). MAX_JOBS still caps the wave size (0 = uncapped); re-invocation drains a dependency graph wave by wave.

### Features

## [0.2.0](https://github.com/jordansmall/spindrift/compare/v0.1.3...v0.2.0) (2026-07-09)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **cli:** `spindrift engage <issue>` is removed. Use `spindrift recover <issue>` instead. This removal targets v0.2.0 as previously announced.
